# Four road distances: all 12 plans

# 5K · 0 weekday workouts: every day of the 12-week plan

Generated directly from the current production engine. 2026-09-21 to 2026-12-13. **50 km/week, 10 km recent long run, five current/running days, 0 weekday workouts selected.**

All seven days are shown below. WORKOUT means a sustained weekday quality session; the LONG run is counted separately. Strides are not counted as a full workout. Race day is excluded from training totals. A dash in the long-run column means no session is labelled long; it does not mean there is no running.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

Engine contract checks: **PASS**. This tests software behavior; policy questions and separate audit failures are recorded in the audit summary.

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

Feasibility status: forecast.

Race day is excluded from training totals. A dash in the long-run column means no session is labelled long; it does not mean there is no running.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.

## Input and generated notes

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

Feasibility status: forecast.

# 10K · 1 weekday workouts: every day of the 12-week plan

Generated directly from the current production engine. 2026-09-21 to 2026-12-13. **50 km/week, 12 km recent long run, five current/running days, 1 weekday workouts selected.**

All seven days are shown below. WORKOUT means a sustained weekday quality session; the LONG run is counted separately. Strides are not counted as a full workout. Race day is excluded from training totals. A dash in the long-run column means no session is labelled long; it does not mean there is no running.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

Engine contract checks: **PASS**. This tests software behavior; policy questions and separate audit failures are recorded in the audit summary.

## Input and generated notes

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

Feasibility status: forecast.

Generated directly from the current production engine. 2026-09-21 to 2026-12-13. **50 km/week, 12 km recent long run, five current/running days, 2 weekday workouts selected.**

All seven days are shown below. WORKOUT means a sustained weekday quality session; the LONG run is counted separately. Strides are not counted as a full workout. Race day is excluded from training totals. A dash in the long-run column means no session is labelled long; it does not mean there is no running.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

## Input and generated notes

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

Feasibility status: forecast.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

## Input and generated notes

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

Feasibility status: forecast.

# Half-marathon · 1 weekday workouts: every day of the 12-week plan

Generated directly from the current production engine. 2026-09-21 to 2026-12-13. **55 km/week, 16 km recent long run, five current/running days, 1 weekday workouts selected.**

All seven days are shown below. WORKOUT means a sustained weekday quality session; the LONG run is counted separately. Strides are not counted as a full workout. Race day is excluded from training totals. A dash in the long-run column means no session is labelled long; it does not mean there is no running.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

Engine contract checks: **PASS**. This tests software behavior; policy questions and separate audit failures are recorded in the audit summary.

Generated directly from the current production engine. 2026-09-21 to 2026-12-13. **55 km/week, 16 km recent long run, five current/running days, 2 weekday workouts selected.**

All seven days are shown below. WORKOUT means a sustained weekday quality session; the LONG run is counted separately. Strides are not counted as a full workout. Race day is excluded from training totals. A dash in the long-run column means no session is labelled long; it does not mean there is no running.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.

- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

## Input and generated notes

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Marathon endurance model · 12-week reference, adapted to your dates and current running. Easy endurance comes first. The workouts shown in each week reflect your background, preferences and available time; the reference does not require every type of speed work. Mileage follows your baseline and limits, not a copied schedule.
- Short block · 84 calendar days. Training starts from your current routine and stays within these dates. The phase follows time remaining to race day; starting distances follow your current routine. Race taper is retained; earlier preparation is not squeezed into this block.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

Feasibility status: forecast.

Generated directly from the current production engine. 2026-09-21 to 2026-12-13. **70 km/week, 23 km recent long run, five current/running days, 1 weekday workouts selected.**

All seven days are shown below. WORKOUT means a sustained weekday quality session; the LONG run is counted separately. Strides are not counted as a full workout. Race day is excluded from training totals. A dash in the long-run column means no session is labelled long; it does not mean there is no running.

For timed or mixed workouts, plan kilometres are allocation estimates, not a distance stopping rule. Follow the saved steps: distance steps stop at metres, timed steps stop at duration. Each session’s total time includes warm-up and cooldown. Easy-day fractional allocations are printed as generated; the whole-kilometre progression requirement applies to long runs.

## Input and generated notes

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Marathon endurance model · 12-week reference, adapted to your dates and current running. Easy endurance comes first. The workouts shown in each week reflect your background, preferences and available time; the reference does not require every type of speed work. Mileage follows your baseline and limits, not a copied schedule.
- Short block · 84 calendar days. Training starts from your current routine and stays within these dates. The phase follows time remaining to race day; starting distances follow your current routine. Race taper is retained; earlier preparation is not squeezed into this block.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

## Input and generated notes

Saved running days: Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Sunday. Long-run day: Sunday. Weekday limit: 120 min. Long-run limit: 300 min. Established routine; recent quality history: two sessions / 40 work minutes per week. Gradual volume, balanced difficulty, varied workout recipes.

Benchmark: 10 km in 50:00 on 1 September 2026, road race. Declared easy pace: 6:00/km. The app funds time at its slower applicable easy target. This is synthetic input, not the user’s saved profile.

- Easy and long runs use distance targets when their structure supports them. Time estimates help you plan; run-walk, recoveries and timed quality sessions keep their prescribed durations.
- Marathon endurance model · 12-week reference, adapted to your dates and current running. Easy endurance comes first. The workouts shown in each week reflect your background, preferences and available time; the reference does not require every type of speed work. Mileage follows your baseline and limits, not a copied schedule.
- Short block · 84 calendar days. Training starts from your current routine and stays within these dates. The phase follows time remaining to race day; starting distances follow your current routine. Race taper is retained; earlier preparation is not squeezed into this block.
- Progression is a forecast. Missed sessions are never added to later workouts.
- Independent training rules; coaching review and real-watch validation are still pending.

Feasibility status: forecast.
